Follow-ups tracked separately: the setup wizard owning its own `<Dialog>` (a UX call — it would stack over the sync panel rather than replace it), and the three counted backlogs the guards now hold. Three debts the guards surfaced are recorded as per-file COUNTS that may shrink and never grow — 9 hardcoded error fallbacks (that IS the v1.3.1 audit), 5 centred coloured status lines on non-settings surfaces, ~20 surviving `#DD4444` (change 17's unfinished business).
- [x] Worker test-infra migration: vitest 4 + vitest-pool-workers 0.18 + wrangler 4 `chore` `#marketplace` `#worker` (added 2026-07-24, from the dependabot triage — blocks 3 open PRs) **DONE 2026-09-01** — wecoded-marketplace PR #80 — vitest 4.1 + pool-workers 0.22 + wrangler 4; storage isolation restored per test via `reset()` in `test/setup.ts`; 304 tests green; deploy workflow on Node 22.
  Three marketplace dependabot PRs (#60, #61, #63) all fail `npm ci` with ERESOLVE and **none of them can be merged as a dependency bump** — together they're one coordinated migration. Verified by building it in a scratch worktree 2026-07-24, not inferred from the peer ranges.
  **What's actually blocking.** #61 takes vitest 2.1.9 → 4.1.10 alone, but `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers@0.5.41` peer-wants `vitest 2.0.x - 2.1.x`. #60 takes pool-workers → 0.18.8, which peer-wants `vitest ^4.1.0`. So they're complementary halves — **but installing both together still doesn't work**: pool-workers 0.18.8 **deletes the `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers/config` subpath export entirely**, and `worker/vitest.config.ts:1` imports `defineWorkersConfig` from exactly that path. 0.18.8's root export is `cloudflarePool` / `cloudflareTest` / `readD1Migrations` — `defineWorkersConfig` no longer exists at all, so `npm test` dies at config load with `Missing "./config" specifier`. It also drags in `wrangler@4.114.0` transitively while `worker/package.json` pins `^3.80.0`, leaving two wranglers in the tree.
  **So the real unit of work is:** rewrite `worker/vitest.config.ts` to the vitest-4 pool API (the package ships a codemod at `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers/codemods/vitest-v3-to-v4`), bump wrangler 3 → 4, and re-verify the D1 migration harness (`readD1Migrations` + the `TEST_MIGRATIONS` binding + `environment: "test"`) still works. Doing the wrangler 4 bump here also unblocks **#63** (`@cloudflare/workers-types` 4 → 5, which fails because `wrangler@3.114.17` peerOptional-wants `^4.20250408.0`) — one migration clears all three PRs.
  **Why it deserves care rather than a quick bump:** `worker-deploy.yml` runs the test suite, then applies D1 migrations, then deploys to production on every push to master. A silently-degraded test harness doesn't just go red — it stops gating a live deploy.
- [x] Project View: merge Artifacts + All files into one Files tab `feature` `#project-view` (added 2026-07-23, **SHIPPED 2026-07-24 — youcoded PR #247, merge `b0f990b9`**)
  Destin's ask: the Artifacts tab looked like a subset of All files, so having both made no sense. **It wasn't a subset** — four things live only on the tracked side: external pins (`kind: 'external'`), version history (discovered files get `versions: []`, `project-file-discovery.ts:122`), deleted/orphan records, and gated roots (a home-dir project skips the disk scan entirely but still lists artifacts, `ipc-handlers.ts:3016`). So this is a merge, not a deletion.
  Shape: one `Files` tab (segments 4→3) = the on-disk `Project Files` walk only, in-folder artifacts **undifferentiated** (the disk is the truth). `+ Add file` becomes a native-picker **Move/Copy import** into the browsed folder (new `artifacts:import-file`; verify-before-unlink so a cross-filesystem move can't eat the source; Replace/Keep both/Skip asked once per batch, disclosed-collisions gate so an undisclosed collision falls back to keep-both; self-import guard so Move+Replace onto a file's own path can't delete it).
  **DESCOPED after Destin tested the branch (2026-07-23):** the design also added an `External Artifacts` section (sidecar records outside the folder, via a flipped `visible-artifacts.ts` rule 4 that dropped the pin requirement). Against his real sidecar it was ~95% incidental noise (163 scratchpad temps, 37 other-device Windows paths, 19 `.claude/` internals; `manualIncludes`=0). Removed; rule 4 reverted to "externals hidden unless pinned". Externals live in the Session Drawer per session — their correct home. The section was Claude's idea and it did not earn its place; the lesson is in the spec amendment banner.
  **"Show deleted" leaves Project View but STAYS in the session drawer** — `VersionEvent` has no content field (`shared/artifacts/types.ts:14`), so a deleted record is a tombstone, not a recovery path; but seeing everything Claude did in a session, deletions included, is the drawer's whole purpose. The flag is synced cross-device (`theme-context.tsx:526`), so a naive cleanup would drop a real preference — pinned by a guard test.
  **Desktop-only in practice:** every `artifacts:*` channel on Android is a `not-implemented-on-mobile` stub (`SessionService.kt:3585-3601`) — mobile Project View is v2, so the shared renderer shows the tab with no data behind it. Ships with an Android stub + a Move gate only. Rider, off the critical path: Android's picker renames every selection to `<timestamp>.<ext>` (`MainActivity.kt:56-64`), so chat attachments are all named `1784838134583.png` — fixed via `OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME` in its own revertible commit.
  Spec: docs/active/specs/2026-07-23-project-view-file-merge-design.md · Plan: docs/active/plans/2026-07-23-project-view-file-merge-plan.md
- [x] "Send Anyway" override on the pending-prompt send block `feature` `#renderer` (added 2026-07-16; shipped 2026-07-17, youcoded PR #165 merge `49bcf49b`)
  Carried out of the fixed "stale toast blocks send" bug (Destin's ask, recorded 2026-07-16): when the send gate claims Claude is waiting on a prompt, a human who can see there's no live prompt should be able to force the send rather than being locked out. Needs design care rather than a bare bypass — a forced send while an Ink menu IS live writes `text + \r` into the menu and can silently select an option; the affordance should probably interrupt/dismiss the pending state first or route through terminal view with a warning. **SHIPPED as ESC-first (Destin chose this over just-unlock):** a refused typed send now surfaces a "Send anyway" button on the toast (App `ToastState` gained an optional `{message, action}` form; string form preserved so the ~8 plain callers are untouched). Clicking it presses ESC in the PTY first — closes any genuinely-live Ink menu, harmless no-op on an idle bar, so it can NEVER answer a real menu — then re-sends past the gate via a `force` flag threaded through InputBar `send`/`sendMessage`. Scoped to the typed-message path (InputBar); programmatic sends (`/model`, `/sync`) keep the plain toast; the minimal touch bar (no handler) falls back to the plain toast. NOT unit-pinned — no InputBar test harness exists — typecheck-verified only; flagged to Destin for a visual eyeball on the next build. Known trade-off he accepted: clicking it while a REAL menu is up cancels that menu (the safe failure).
- [x] Chat view momentum ("flick") scrolling `feature` `#renderer` (added 2026-07-17, shipped 2026-07-17 youcoded PR #169 merge `f71c5f39`)
  Destin's ask: after flicking the trackpad, the chat should keep scrolling and decelerate at a reasonable rate; today it stops the instant the finger lifts. SHIPPED with a "tap to catch the glide" refinement (any touch while coasting freezes it in place, incl. sub-pixel resting-finger jitter). Verified in a dev instance. Root cause: the existing custom wheel handler (`ChatView.tsx:344-393`) hijacks native scroll via non-passive `preventDefault` + `scrollBy(deltaY * multiplier)` with burst-acceleration but NO inertia — and on Linux libinput emits no momentum tail (unlike macOS's ~20-30-event tail the handler's comment assumes), so scrolling dies on finger-lift. Fix = velocity-tracked `requestAnimationFrame` glide-out with friction deceleration, fed by the wheel deltas; cancel the glide on new wheel input / pointerdown / arrow-key scroll / unmount, and stop at scroll boundaries. Gate momentum to trackpad pixel-mode (`deltaMode===0`) so discrete mouse-wheel stays snappy. Shared renderer code but inert on Android (no wheel events on touch) — must not disturb the atBottom auto-scroll effects (208-266) or `scrollToBottom`. Feel is timing-sensitive → tune constants then hand Destin a dev instance to eyeball the deceleration rate.
- [x] Right-click context menu (copy/paste/context-aware + Ask about this + file-pill menu) `feature` `#renderer` `#ui` (added 2026-07-17, shipped 2026-07-17 youcoded PR #169 merge `f71c5f39` — image sub-menu split to its own item below)
  Destin's ask: standard right-click behavior for copy/paste "and other standard left-click shit." Today there is NO context menu anywhere in the app (no `onContextMenu`, no Electron `Menu`, no `contextmenu` listener — verified 2026-07-17); right-click does nothing. Decision (Destin, 2026-07-17): CUSTOM THEMED React menu (not native Electron menu) — app fonts/colors/glass blur, follows active theme + wallpaper, cross-platform (desktop + Android long-press + remote browser). Built on the app's real `.layer-surface` popover, styled like `components/ui/Select.tsx` option rows (text-2xs, text-fg-2, hover bg-inset, accent-icon primary items). Context-AWARE scope per target: selected read-only text → Ask about this · Copy · Select all; code block → Ask about this · Copy code block · Copy · Select all; link → Open link · Copy link address · Copy · Select all; image → Ask about this · Copy image · Save image as… · Copy image address; editable input bar → Cut · Copy · Paste · Select all (Cut/Copy/Paste/Select-All disabled-not-hidden when unavailable). Reuse existing primitives: `navigator.clipboard`, `IPC.CLIPBOARD_SAVE_IMAGE`, `window.claude.shell.showItemInFolder`/`openPath` (preload.ts:677/681). **"Ask about this"** (text/code/image): drop the target into the input bar with a follow-up scaffold ("In an earlier message, you said: \"…\".\n\nThe user has a follow-up: ") and park the cursor — simple v1 = plain prompt text, no new plumbing (see separate roadmap item for the native version). **File-pill menu** (NEW, Destin 2026-07-17): right-click on the file pills/buttons in chat tool cards (`tool-views/ToolBody.tsx:151`, which already OPEN on left-click) → Open file · View in folder · Copy file name · Copy as path. Kept on right-click to preserve left-click open; confirm with Destin if he wants it on left-click instead. Mockup approved (artifact 2a6ebbe2 rev 2; sparkle icon chosen for Ask-about-this). **IMPLEMENTED (pending Destin's dev review), branch `feat/chat-flick-scroll`:** new `components/context-menu/` module — `ContextMenuHost` (one document-level capture `contextmenu` listener, mounted in App.tsx by RemoteSnapshotExporter), `build-menu.ts` (pure DOM/selection → entries, scoped to `.chat-scroll` + `.input-bar-textarea` — later extended to `[data-artifact-viewer]` + `.artifact-edit-textarea`, see the artifact-viewer item below), `ContextMenu.tsx` (portalled `.layer-surface` L4 popover, edge-flip, arrow-key nav, Esc/click-outside/scroll dismiss), `menu-icons.tsx`, `clipboard.ts` (secure-context writeText + execCommand fallback for insecure remote). Ask-about-this reuses a new `youcoded:compose-insert` window CustomEvent (InputBar prepends scaffold + caret). File pills got `data-file-path` (absolute `fp`) in ToolBody.tsx; desktop-gated Open/Reveal via existing shell IPC. **DEFERRED — image sub-menu (Copy image / Save image / Copy address):** chat/artifact images are ephemeral `blob:` URLs and there is NO copy-image-to-clipboard or save-image-to-disk IPC (only `clipboard:save-image` which READS the OS clipboard). Doing it right needs 2 new main IPC handlers + bytes plumbing (`artifacts.readBinary`) + `data-artifact-path` on ImageView/ArtifactThumbnail imgs + 3-surface parity + won't work on Android/remote — split out below. Typecheck clean; not unit-pinned (no host for these renderer interactions) — verified by dev review.
- [x] Agent-shell Bash CWD — scoped persistence (Claude Code's model) `feature` `#native-runtime` `#harness` (added 2026-07-17, shipped 2026-07-18 — youcoded PR #174)
  Today every Bash tool call is a **fresh process** started at the session cwd: `cd` in one call does NOT carry to the next, and nothing tells the model that — verified 2026-07-17 (PID changed `1042732 → 1042910` across two calls). Observed cost in one session: ~6 failed tool calls (`node_modules/.bin/vitest` "not found", `git add` "pathspec did not match") before the model diagnosed that CWD simply never persists. This is a known, contested design point in agentic harnesses — Claude Code issues #42837, #35058, #28228, #22023, #37659 all debate it, and the #1 complaint across them is *silent* behavior (the model can't tell which cwd model it's in and guesses wrong). Three models in the wild: persistent process (Anthropic API Bash tool), scoped persistence (Claude Code CLI default — cwd persists only within approved dirs, `cd` outside reverted with a `Shell cwd was reset to …` notice), and stateless/always-reset (many CI harnesses). YouCoded is a fourth, undocumented case (fresh process, never persists, no notice). **Destin's call (2026-07-17): adopt scoped persistence.** Concretely: track cwd in the harness and pass it as `cwd:` to each spawned command, updating it when the command changes dir (the robust detection trick is appending `; printf '\n__YC_CWD__%s' "$PWD"` and parsing the sentinel back out of stdout); scope it to the session root (`ctx.cwd`) — a `cd` resolving outside is reverted + a reset notice appended; persist only cwd, not env (match Claude Code); update the Bash tool description to state the rules plainly. Cheapest 80% fallback if full persistence is deferred: keep the stateless process but emit the reset notice + an explicit tool-description line ("each Bash call starts fresh at the workspace root; use absolute paths or `cd X && …` in one command"). Companion bug half (Grep `spawn ENOTDIR`) — PR #172's `cwd:` fix was a MISDIAGNOSIS: the bug kept reproducing in the packaged app. Real root cause found + shipped 2026-07-20 — youcoded PR #194: `@vscode/ripgrep`'s `rgPath` resolves INSIDE `app.asar` (a file), so `spawn()` of that command path throws `ENOTDIR` SYNCHRONOUSLY (before the 'error' handler can attach); the fix rewrites rgPath to `app.asar.unpacked` (mirroring the pty-worker pattern) + try/catches the sync throw in both Grep and Bash. Full investigation + research: `docs/archive/investigations/2026-07-17-agent-shell-cwd-and-grep-enotdir.md`.
- [x] Right-click menu — artifact viewer "Ask about this" (file + line-number reference) `feature` `#renderer` `#ui` (added 2026-07-19, shipped 2026-07-19 youcoded PR #186 merge `a0478dea`)
  Destin's ask (2026-07-19): the same "Ask about this" affordance the chat right-click menu has, but for text documents in the artifact viewer, with a scaffold naming the file and the referenced lines — `The user is referencing lines 12-18 from "docs/notes.md". Respond to the following prompt accordingly:` then the caret. Scope decisions (Destin, 2026-07-19): **line numbers with quote fallback** (not quote-only), and **both** CodeView and MarkdownView (all plain-text-backed artifacts). Implementation: `buildContextMenu` grew a `[data-artifact-viewer]` branch checked BEFORE the `.chat-scroll` gate (the viewer lives outside chat); CodeView + MarkdownView tag their content container with `data-artifact-viewer` / `data-doc-path` / `data-artifact-source`. `data-artifact-source` is `raw` when the `<pre>` is a verbatim copy of the file (code files, `.txt`) and `rendered` for parsed markdown — line numbers are only computed for `raw`, because rendered markdown prose has no 1:1 source mapping, and `rendered` always falls back to a quoted excerpt. Line lookup is first-occurrence `indexOf` over the `<pre>`'s **`textContent`** (NOT `innerText`: innerText is layout-dependent, would count soft-wrap breaks as source newlines on the `whitespace-pre-wrap` `.txt` `<pre>`, forces a reflow, and isn't implemented by jsdom). Reuses the existing `youcoded:compose-insert` CustomEvent — no new plumbing. Two deliberate behaviors: **no selection → no "Ask about this"** (unlike chat's whole-bubble fallback, since falling back to a whole file would paste the entire document), and the path cited is project-relative (reads better in a prompt than an absolute path). Also fixed in passing: the artifact **edit-mode textarea** now gets the Cut/Copy/Paste menu via a new `.artifact-edit-textarea` class — Electron ships no default context menu, so right-click there previously did nothing at all. Pinned by `desktop/src/renderer/components/context-menu/build-menu.test.tsx` (6 tests — the module had no test file before this). **Note for the image sub-menu item below:** this feature uses `data-doc-path`, deliberately NOT `data-artifact-path`, which stays reserved for that item's absolute-path-on-`<img>` tagging.
- [x] **Artifact pane → credible code editor (Tier 1 workstream — one PR)** `feature` `#renderer` `#ui` `#artifacts` (added 2026-07-20, shipped 2026-07-22 youcoded PR #200 merge `1cf9cbf2` — items 1/2/3/5 + review fixes + drawer icon refresh; item 4 search split to the follow-up below)
  Destin's ask (2026-07-20): make YouCoded useful for real developer work, not just non-developers, starting with how code is edited/managed in the artifact viewer. Five changes that are cheap because the substrate already exists; scoped as ONE workstream/PR because they all land in the same three files and splitting them would mean three rounds of the same regression testing. **Positioning caveat, decided before building:** `docs/archive/specs/2026-07-09-platform-vision-roadmap.md:139` positions the app as "the open, personal Cowork" with non-developer accessibility as core identity, and its competitive matrix (L112–139) has NO row for an editor, file tree, or diff view — the named gaps are multi-model freedom, approval inbox, memory. So this is a deliberate direction change, not a gap-fill. The framing chosen is **not** IDE parity (unwinnable against Cursor on its own turf) but *"I can trust and steer what the agent did to my code"* — review, diff, navigate, small surgical edits. That keeps the non-developer audience intact; every item below is justified against it, and anything that only serves IDE parity belongs in Tier 2/3 items below, not here.
  **(1) Unlock code editing.** Editability is gated by a hard-coded extension allowlist at `artifact-views/ActiveArtifactView.tsx:48` (`md | markdown | txt`) — that one line is the ONLY thing blocking it. The save path underneath already handles arbitrary paths: `window.claude.artifacts.save` → `preload.ts:1234` → `ipc-handlers.ts:3094`, atomic `.tmp`+rename (`:3113-3114`), sidecar version event, `artifacts:changed` broadcast, path-traversal guard at `:3135-3138`. Conflict handling already exists too (`ActiveArtifactView.tsx:76-88` + banner at `:155-179`). Invert the allowlist to a binary-file denylist rather than enumerating code extensions. Today a developer literally cannot fix a typo in a `.ts` file without leaving the app.
  **(2) Replace CodeView with CodeMirror 6.** `artifact-views/CodeView.tsx` is 21 lines and does not render code — it wraps the file in a markdown fence and hands it to the chat renderer (`:9-11`), so highlighting goes through a full react-markdown + rehype-highlight parse per render (`MarkdownContent.tsx:15,234`). No line numbers, no gutter, no folding, and it re-parses the whole document on every keystroke once (1) lands. **CM6 over Monaco specifically:** Android runs this exact React bundle in a WebView (`WebViewHost.kt:160`), and Monaco is heavy, worker-dependent, and touch-hostile; CM6 is ~150KB tree-shaken and works on touch. Hard constraint for whatever ships: it must load from the asset origin and go through `window.claude.artifacts.*` — anything Node-only or `file://`-dependent breaks Android (see `useArtifactBytes.ts:20-23`). The single biggest perceived-quality jump on this list.
  **(3) Use the `diff` library already in package.json.** `diff@^9.0.0` + `@types/diff` are declared but **nothing in the renderer imports them** — both diff renderers are hand-rolled. `ToolBody.tsx` prefers Claude Code's `structuredPatch` (`:331-343`) but falls back to its own O(m·n) LCS (`diffLines()` at `:260-289`); and the edit-conflict "diff" at `ActiveArtifactView.tsx:181-192` isn't a diff at all, just two `<pre>` columns side by side (the comment at `:154` already flags that a real diff library was deferred). Swap in jsdiff and unify both call sites on one component. Keep `DIFF_PREVIEW_LINES` capping behavior.
  **(4) Cross-file content search.** `@vscode/ripgrep@^1.18.0` is already a dependency but is wired ONLY to the agent's Grep tool (`main/harness/tools/grep.ts:3,35`) — no renderer caller. The UI can currently only filter by *filename*: `FilesTab.tsx:226-227` matches file names and explicitly not folder names, `SessionDrawer.tsx:360-370` is a name search, and `ContentFindBar.tsx` is within-the-open-document only. One IPC channel exposing the existing binary gets project-wide content search nearly free. Needs 3-surface parity (preload / remote-shim / `SessionService.kt`) per the usual rule — and note Android has no ripgrep binary, so it needs a Kotlin-side fallback or an honest unsupported notice.
  **(5) Watch the project directory.** There is no watcher on the project dir — `artifacts:changed` is push-broadcast from write sites (`ipc-handlers.ts:2879,2899,2915,3125,3142,3201,3225`), so an external change (a `git checkout`, an edit in another editor, a rebase) leaves the pane showing stale content with no indication. `chokidar@^4.0.3` is already a dep and already used this way for themes (`main/theme-watcher.ts:53`) and sync-spaces (`main/sync-spaces/engine.ts:83`) — point it at the project root and reuse the existing broadcast. Watch out for the debounce/cost profile on large repos (respect the existing cap-truncation in `project-file-discovery.ts`) and for a save-triggers-watcher-triggers-reload loop against (1)'s edit buffer.
  **SPEC: `docs/archive/specs/2026-07-20-artifact-pane-code-editor.md`** (2026-07-20) — read §2 before implementing. Speccing surfaced four pre-existing defects the 3-extension allowlist has been masking, and the fixes are IN scope, not follow-ups: (a) **the conflict banner is unreachable dead code** — `ActiveArtifactView.tsx:80` filters `evt.by === 'agent'` but every emitter in `ipc-handlers.ts` hardcodes `by: 'user'`, and with no filesystem watcher an agent CLI write emits nothing at all, so the "Claude also edited this file" safety net has never fired; (b) **no dirty tracking anywhere** — switching artifacts silently discards the draft, and the `:71-73` force-exit exists to close a window where a save during the `content === null` fetch transient would truncate a file to empty (the highest-risk regression in the work); (c) **no size cap on `artifacts:get`** — a naked `readFile(utf8)` with no stat/sniff/cap, so a 5MB file blocks the main thread twice over; (d) **save is unconditional last-write-wins** with no mtime/hash token and a console-only error path. Net effect: the watcher (item 5) is the PREREQUISITE for unlocking editing (item 1), not an independent nice-to-have. Four decisions taken 2026-07-20 (spec §3): **D1** syntax colors DERIVED from existing tokens (`--accent`/`--fg-2`/`--link`/`--code`) the way `theme-engine.ts:239-245` already derives `--code`, rather than a new per-theme token family — no `wecoded-themes` authoring story or back-fill; **D2** cross-file search is desktop-only with the existing `remote-unsupported.ts` notice + a ~3-line Kotlin stub (the `project:*` precedent), not a divergent Kotlin reimplementation; **D3** unsaved changes get a Save/Discard/Cancel prompt, NOT auto-save (which would write real source files with no explicit action and race the agent); **D4** any text file is editable with a binary denylist, matching `project-file-discovery.ts:18-22`'s deliberate no-allowlist stance — this also fixes CodeView's 9-extension gap (`rs`/`go`/`java`/`sh`/`kt`/`sql`/`toml`/`c`/`cpp` currently fall to BinaryFallback) in the same change. Spec §9 gives the in-PR sequencing (watcher first, CM6 late, search cuttable to a follow-up). Expect `build-menu.test.tsx` to go red — the right-click menu keys off `<pre>`/`data-artifact-source` and `.artifact-edit-textarea`, and a CM6 editor is neither; re-point the branches, don't weaken the tests.
  **AMENDED 2026-07-20 (consequence review — spec §12). D5 RESOLVED 2026-07-22 — no longer blocked; implementation plan: `docs/archive/plans/2026-07-22-artifact-pane-code-editor-implementation.md`.** A review pass found the workstream as first specced ships a security regression, and that four sections of the spec were wrong (all corrected in place, marked `CORRECTED 2026-07-20`). The blocking finding: **D4 removes the only barrier in front of arbitrary file writes.** `artifacts:get`/`save` have a path-traversal check and NOTHING else (`ipc-handlers.ts:3019-3027`, `:3134-3139`) — only `read-binary` carries a sensitive-path deny-list (`read-binary-access.ts:62-71`, which blocks `.ssh`/`.aws`/`.env*` etc). So one channel refuses to READ `.env` while another will happily OVERWRITE it. `artifactId` is a raw relative path and the discovery pass's dot-dir skip is a listing filter the IPC never consults, so lifting the renderer gate exposes `.git/hooks/pre-commit` (code execution on next commit), `.claude/settings.json` + `.claude/hooks/*` (hook config IS command execution), `.env`, `CLAUDE.md`, and `.youcoded/artifacts.json` — the last of which escalates OUT of the project root, because the tracked save branch writes `artifact.absolutePath!` with no traversal check at all (`:3110-3112`). Not a regression the workstream introduces (main never enforced anything; the renderer allowlist was load-bearing security by accident) but it hands the UI a live path to all of it. **The predicate must live in MAIN, not the renderer** — the first draft put it in `ActiveArtifactView.tsx` and called it "the security boundary," which was exactly backwards. **NEW DECISION D5 NEEDED before implementing:** per-path policy for `.git/` `.claude/` `.youcoded/` `.env*` `CLAUDE.md` — not editable / editable-behind-confirm / free. *(Resolved 2026-07-22 after verifying all §12.1 claims against master: `.git/`, `.youcoded/`, and the read-binary sensitive set minus dotenv are never editable, checked in MAIN against the resolved absolute path; `.claude/` and `.env*`/`.envrc` editable behind a confirm with a main-required `confirmed` flag; `CLAUDE.md` free. Table + refusal UX in the plan §1, spec §3/D5 updated.)* Other corrections: **(§5.3)** the draft said to expect `build-menu.test.tsx` to go red — the opposite is true and it is the dangerous direction, the test hand-builds a synthetic `<pre>` and never mounts CodeView (`build-menu.test.tsx:10-19`) so it stays GREEN while the feature breaks; and CM6 virtualization makes the `indexOf`-over-`textContent` line-number technique emit *plausible wrong* citations (a selection at line 800 reports "line 41") into prompt scaffolds, so it must move to `view.state.doc.lineAt()` and the test must be rebuilt around the real component — §5 is meaningfully larger than a viewer swap. **(§8.2)** a filesystem watcher CANNOT emit `by: 'agent'` — a `git checkout`/`npm install`/formatter would all be labeled "Claude also edited this file", a guessed cause in a user-facing string; emit `by: 'external'` and reword the banner + its three actions. **(§8.5)** there is no per-window active project — `projectRoot` derives per-session from `session.cwd` (`App.tsx:1328`), sessions detach freely across an uncapped number of windows, and one window can show two projects at once; the watcher belongs in main, refcounted by `projectRoot`, following the `topicWatchers` shape (`ipc-handlers.ts:2253`) and its documented leak trap (`:2314-2315`), NOT per-window and NOT leader-owned. Also newly captured: **§2.4's last-write-wins defect was identified in the first draft and then never scheduled a fix** (now §9 step 2 — round-trip an mtime/hash through get→save, or D3's prompt sits on top of a save that still silently clobbers the agent's work, which is exactly what "Keep mine" does by design); capture-phase key handlers guard on `tagName === 'TEXTAREA'` and won't recognize CM6's contenteditable, so **Shift+Space would cycle the model and Shift+Tab would reach the PTY** from inside the editor (`App.tsx:1806-1817`, `:2419-2429`); Android soft-keyboard support is NEW work not preservation (the artifact pane has zero `--vvp-offset` consumers today, and `adjustNothing` + `overlays-content` is precisely the contract CM6's `scrollIntoView` assumptions break against); and search has nowhere to land until a jump-to-line concept exists (zero hits for `scrollIntoView`/`gotoLine` in `artifact-views/`; selection is keyed by artifactId not path, but `FilepathToken.tsx:84-130` already implements the path→id upsert resolution to reuse). Two findings deliberately NOT scheduled in this workstream and filed separately below: the write-guard bypass and unbounded sidecar growth.
  **STATUS 2026-07-22: steps 1–5 implemented — youcoded PR #200 open** (watcher, D5 boundary + Kotlin mirror, jsdiff, D4 unlock + dirty tracking, CM6). Cross-file search (item 4) cut at the plan's cut line to a follow-up PR; md/txt kept the textarea edit path. Kotlin tests gated on CI (local JDK 26 incompatible). Destin's eyeball items: editor feel, syntax colors (Crème + a wallpaper theme), diff readability, confirm wording, Android device pass.
  **Verification note:** per the live-app-safety rule this is dev-instance work (`bash scripts/run-dev.sh`), and per CLAUDE.md's 2026-07-16 lesson the final "does the editor feel right" pass is Destin's eyeball, not a scripted CDP rig. DOM-level assertions (line numbers present, save fires, diff rows correct) should still be unit-pinned — `build-menu.test.tsx` is the precedent for testing this area.
- [x] Cross-file content search (cut from the Tier 1 code-editor PR) `feature` `#renderer` `#artifacts` (added 2026-07-22, shipped 2026-07-22 youcoded PR #205 merge `813a6c83`)
  Shipped per the archived plan step 6 + in-review design decisions (Destin, 2026-07-22): NO name/contents toggle — one search box, two labeled sections ("Matches by file name (N)" cards, then "Matches by file contents (N)"); content hits GROUPED per file, groups collapsible and collapsed by default, sorted by match count desc with the display cap keeping whole groups; no prose empty state in search mode (the (0) counts carry it). Engine: `artifacts:search-content` over bundled ripgrep `--json`, literal `-F -i` query, caps 20/file + 200 total + 1MB stdout + 5s kill, desktop-only (Kotlin stub, remote unsupported → names-only). Jump-to-hit via `revealLine(n)` on ActiveArtifactHandle (CM6 dispatch, retries across the lazy-chunk mount) — the reusable open-at-line primitive the future git surface needs. Real-ripgrep e2e test pins literal/case/exclusion/truncation behavior.
- [x] Artifact pane: real loading state instead of the "no longer on disk" flash `bug` `#renderer` `#artifacts` (added 2026-07-22, **FIXED 2026-08-12 — youcoded PR #303, merge `1b69194e`**: `content === null` split into a four-state `ArtifactContentState` (loading/ready/missing/error — the fourth mandated by the no-misleading-errors rule: read errors now surface accurately instead of as "no longer on disk"), owned by a new shared `useArtifactContent` hook that replaced two near-duplicate host effects; loading renders the house `LoadingState` primitive. The review-round delta also fixed a recreated-file stuck-notice regression and pinned the stale-resolve race. **Destin: eyeball the brief "Loading file…" spinner on artifact open** — the alternative (blank pane during the blip) is a two-line change if you prefer it. Follow-up filed: binary-sniffed text-extension files render a blank pane.)
  Viewers show "This file is no longer on disk." during the content-fetch transient (content===null covers both loading AND orphan). Pre-existing (MarkdownView always did it), more visible now that every file type opens in the pane. Needs an explicit loading vs orphan signal from the hosts.
- [x] Git surface in-app — diff vs HEAD, stage, commit, branch `feature` `#renderer` `#ui` `#git` (added 2026-07-20; SHIPPED 2026-07-23 — youcoded PR #213 merged to master. Per-file review in the SessionDrawer: footer `+N −N` entry, pushed review view with uncommitted card + rename-following commit timeline, real-index stage/unstage, commit composer, revert-with-confirm. Desktop-only; Android/remote degrade to `not-implemented-on-mobile`. Branch ops + push/PR are the separate phase-2 entry below. Spec/plan archived to `docs/archive/{specs,plans}/2026-07-22-git-surface.md` — the spec's §2 UI is superseded in places by Destin's live-testing revisions, recorded in its "Live-iteration amendments" section)
  **The highest-value developer feature on this list and the real differentiator** — reviewing what the agent just did is the core anxiety of using an agent-first tool, and right now the only in-app view of a change is the per-tool-call diff inside a chat card (`ToolBody.tsx:318`). There is no way to see the cumulative working-tree diff, no staging, no commit, no branch awareness. Git currently appears in this codebase only as *plumbing* — the sync transport (`main/sync-spaces/git-transport.ts`) — and never as a user-facing surface. Sequencing note: `ROADMAP.md:43` already plans to drop the hand-installed `gh` dependency in favor of direct GitHub REST, which is adjacent work and should probably be settled first so this doesn't build on a dependency that's being removed. Recommended to jump the queue ahead of tabs and the file tree once Tier 1 ships.

- [x] `sync-spaces-engine` macOS CI flake — `fs.watch` test still blows the 5s vitest timeout `bug` `#ci` `#sync` (added 2026-07-19)
  **SHIPPED 2026-07-22 — youcoded PR #198 (merge `79ac5802`). This entry's hypothesis was exactly right, and it was a partially-applied fix.** PR #180 replaced this file's six inline `{ timeout: 5000 }` literals with a named `WAIT_MS = 60_000`, but that knob bounds the `vi.waitFor` POLL, not the test. The file had no `vi.setConfig`, so it inherited vitest's default 5000ms `testTimeout` and every 60s wait was really capped at 5s — the ceiling #180 raised was unreachable. Its two sibling files got the setConfig in #163 (`sync-spaces-project-discovery.test.ts:23`, `sync-spaces-git-transport.test.ts:18`); this one was missed. Verified by probe: a file with no setConfig doing `vi.waitFor({timeout: 60_000})` dies with exactly `Test timed out in 5000ms` — the PR #181 macOS message — and with the setConfig added a 7s test in this file passes. **Two knobs are required per file: one bounds the poll, one bounds the test.**
  **Same gap found and closed in two more files** while sweeping: `subagent-watcher.test.ts` and `transcript-watcher.test.ts` both carried `WATCH_MS = 15_000` with no setConfig — i.e. PR #163 was partially applied the same way #180 was (poll ceilings raised in four files, test ceilings in only two). Those are precisely the files the `sync-warnings` entry above named as the family's remaining victims. A repo-wide sweep for "poll ceiling above the file's effective test ceiling" now returns nothing; `sync-spaces-service` and `conversations-service` also lack setConfig but use `vi.waitFor`'s 1000ms default, safely under the 5s budget. The five slowest remaining tests (`conversation-store` 6.0s, `secrets-store` 3.0s, `native-home` 3.0s, `update-installer` 2.0s, `sync-spaces-two-device` 1.0s) all already carry deliberate inline per-test timeouts of 10–30s and are correctly bounded by that other mechanism — do NOT "normalize" them to setConfig without adding one, the inline arg is currently their only knob.
  PR #180 ("one named 60s ceiling for the fs.watch integration waits + macOS CI") reduced but did NOT eliminate this. Hit again on youcoded PR #181: `tests/sync-spaces-engine.test.ts > "still syncs real lockfiles a user keeps in a project (Cargo.lock)"` failed macOS-only with `Test timed out in 5000ms`, then passed cleanly on a plain re-run with no code change. Likely cause: #180 raised the ceiling on the internal *waits* but the per-test vitest `testTimeout` is still 5000ms, so a slow macOS runner blows the test timeout before the wait budget matters. Fix is probably an explicit per-test timeout on the fs.watch integration cases (or a suite-level `testTimeout` for that file), not a longer wait. Costs a CI re-run every time it fires, and trains people to re-run red macOS builds without reading them — which is how a real failure gets merged.
- [x] UI consistency system — shared primitives + the 51-change migration `feature` `#ui` (added 2026-07-16, COMPLETE 2026-07-24)
  Full design settled with Destin 2026-07-16 across 8 interactive workbench sessions; every change approved by number (Session 8 approved in full 2026-07-16 after three render iterations). Scope: `components/ui/` primitives (Button 5 variants × 4 sizes incl. icon + CloseButton, Toggle 36×20 accent w/ bordered knob, TextInput/Textarea/Select with themed dropdown menus — no OS-rendered controls anywhere except the documented color-picker/title= exceptions, Checkbox/Radio, native accent-accent sliders (custom styling rejected — wiring fix only), Loading/Empty/ErrorState family incl. the two-action error card, AnchorTip, Toast, SegmentedTabs (transparent inactive), ProgressBar), card-surface + badge-tone unification, one screen layer (ProjectView z-8000 → z-40) + one exit affordance, named micro type scale (text-2xs/3xs/4xs over ~538 raw px sites), --link + --on-destructive derivation for community packs, creme.json contrast fix (real shipping bug), SessionDrawer adopts ProjectView's FileFilterPopover, games-lobby token migration (Accept = Button primary), coarse-pointer hit areas + hover:hover guards, BrailleSpinner on the model-loading strip, headerless Settings drawer (no title/✕; Esc + click-outside close; macOS traffic-light padding moves to the scroll body) + SettingsRow type/icon bump within the same card size. **Spec with exact class recipes, full ledger, native-control inventory, tranche plan + implementation risks: docs/archive/specs/2026-07-16-ui-consistency-design-spec.md** (artifact links inside). Implement tranche 0 (tokens + primitives + pinning tests + protection-cascade fix) first, then 8 surface-by-surface tranches, worktree + one commit per surface (SettingsRow playbook). Follow-on guardrails (ui-primitives doc, rule updates, audit-ui-tokens pass, ESLint) listed in spec §8, not yet approved. **Update 2026-07-17 — tranches 0+1 SHIPPED (youcoded PR #164, merge `31900a2f`):** tranche 0 = type tokens, `--link`/`--link-hover` + `--on-destructive` derivation, creme.json contrast fix + audit-reads-JSON + pinning test, the protection-cascade fix, and all 14 `components/ui/` primitives with 56 pinning tests; tranche 1 = buttons, changes 1–14. Five spec recipes were WRONG against the real code and shipped corrected — read **spec §10 (implementation log) BEFORE tranches 2–8**, not §1–§9: change 43's contrast arithmetic (white on #DD4444 is 4.213:1, not 4.7 — the stated rule would flip every danger button to near-black at lower contrast), §9.K's "add `.layer-surface .bg-destructive`" (would CREATE the hover bug, not fix it), change 7's pill className override (Tailwind resolves by CSS source order, so the pills silently rendered as rounded rectangles — `buttonClasses()` now merges), the Toggle knob offsets (17/1 with a constant border, not 18/2), and AnchorTip's capture-phase Esc (`useEscClose` is a LIFO stack that already does it, and gets Android hardware-back free). Remaining: tranches 2–8. **Update 2026-07-19 — the button triage §10.7 owed is DONE and the count was wrong:** not ~25–30 hand-rolled buttons but **~153 across ~50 files** (plus 23 icon-only closers and ~52 non-button surfaces that belong to SegmentedTabs/cards, not Button). Triaged into 8 mechanical patterns + 16 judgment calls + the CloseButton sweep = **changes 52–76, all decided by Destin 2026-07-19** — see **spec §11** for the full ledger with per-site recipes, and the workbench artifact <https://claude.ai/code/artifact/d9174f7b-139d-44e6-b009-c2da3e7d80c1>. Two decisions departed from the recommendation and are marked ▲ in §11.3 — do not "fix" them back: **61** keeps the permission prompt's inverted red ("Always allow" stays red even though red means "No" one row below — muscle memory on the app's most-clicked control wins), and **62** gives skip-permissions filled `danger`, accepting that it now looks identical to "Remove project". Also note **change 75**: five buttons ship `hover:bg-accent` over a `bg-accent` base and have no hover feedback today, so tranche 2 changes behavior, not just appearance — call it out in the PR. Still true from before: the `bg-accent text-on-accent` grep also matches the user chat bubble and theme swatches, which must NOT become Buttons. **Update 2026-07-19 — tranche 2's BUTTON half SHIPPED** (youcoded PR #181, merge `2bf29a44`; branch + worktree cleaned up): ~150 buttons across 55 files now go through the primitive, plus the change-76 CloseButton sweep (29 closers, 9 of which had no accessible name at all — now enforced by the type signature). Also found and fixed: **§10.7's "changes 1–14 are done" was false** (AccountSection/LocalModelsSection/SettingsPanel/ConnectGithubModal still held ~21 hand-rolled buttons, none of them in the 153 count — see spec §11.7); **decision 62 missed a third skip-permissions Create button** on the welcome form; and SyncPanel's confirm dialog had an unreachable `confirmColor='blue'` branch (one call site, passes "red") carrying one of the last hardcoded-blue buttons — deleted, not migrated. **All seven open items DECIDED 2026-07-19 (spec §11.8):** A/B/C/F/G shipped in `6bf5e7e2` (Add Device → secondary; ToolCard's AskUserQuestion pair → primary+ghost; the compact split button keeps its joined shape as a documented exception; the attachment ✕ keeps 16px but gains an accessible name; the send button migrates, keeping `bg-accent` because community packs style it through that class). **D and E changed shape and became new changes 77/78** — D is now a general pattern ("fields with a submit/copy put the button INSIDE the field, right-aligned", ~10 sites, needs a new `InputGroup` primitive with `focus-within` on the wrapper, ships WITH change 20 — spec §11.9), E becomes a `Select` under change 21 (spec §11.10). Also found: **Halftone Dimension's `custom_css` has a `.send-btn` rule that matches nothing in the renderer** — the send-button glow comes from its `.bg-accent` rule instead; a `wecoded-themes` fix. Formerly, plus two queued visual checks (the `panel-glass` nav chips and ThemeScreen's "Browse Theme Marketplace" lost their resting fills when decision 60 collapsed the filled-grey family to outline). Tranche 2's TOGGLE half (changes 15–21) is untouched and still open. **Update 2026-07-19 — tranche 2's INPUT half SHIPPED (youcoded PR #183), so TRANCHE 2 IS COMPLETE.** Changes 15–17, 19–21, 42, 77 across 51 files; 2668 tests green. Consumers after the sweep: Toggle 28, TextInput 31, Textarea 9, Select 10, InputGroup 11 across 10 files — before this tranche every one of those primitives had ZERO consumers (they shipped in tranche 0 and sat unused). **New primitive `InputGroup` (change 77)**: it could NOT be a className on TextInput, because plain FIELD puts the border AND `focus:border-accent` on the `<input>` — move the border to a wrapper and the bare input has no border to recolor, so the field would show no focus state at all; the wrapper takes `focus-within:border-accent`. `FIELD_SURFACE`/`FIELD_TEXT` were split out of FIELD so a bordered field and a grouped one can't drift apart. Pinned by tests. **Latent bug fixed en route:** `fieldClasses` CONCATENATED its className while `buttonClasses` has merged since tranche 0 — Tailwind resolves competing utilities by CSS SOURCE order, so a caller's `text-sm`/`px-4` was an accident of emission order, not an override. Identical mechanism to the tranche-0 pills-render-as-rectangles bug (spec §10.3); fields carried it unnoticed because they had no consumers. Now routes through `mergeClasses`, two tests pin it. **Behavior changed, not just appearance — mention on release:** ~20 toggles gained an accessible name (most were bare `<button>`s inside a `<label>`, which names nothing); four `<label>` wrappers became `<div>`+`aria-label` around Select triggers for the same reason; `PerformancePopup`'s GPU control announced as a switch while rendering a 16×16 SQUARE and is now a real toggle. **Spec §11.9 was wrong about three InputGroup sites** — QuickChips is a two-field form with a footer (`addCustom` needs BOTH fields), the add-provider submit sits below three fields, the Android add-device submit below four, and LocalModelsSection has NO endpoint field at all (InputGroup went to its model search, whose clear-X was already an inside-field action faked with `absolute`+`pr-8`). All verified in code, not assumed — same §11.7 lesson. Corrections in spec §13.4. **Found outside the sweep:** `index.tsx` (the remote login screen) still had a hardcoded `bg-blue-600` button and a gray-focus password field — the button half missed it because that sweep covered `components/`, not the renderer root; that was the last hardcoded blue. And `App.tsx:2843` held a THIRD copy of the skip-permissions warning string on a raw `#DD4444` (the toggle above it had been migrated, the text walked past) — same shape as change 62's missed third Create button. All four copies plus the info tooltip heading now ride the destructive token. **Deferred: change 78** (folder picker → Select) — `projectFolder` is a single string and there is no recents list anywhere in the app, so it needs a new settings key + IPC + cap + eviction policy. A feature, not a migration; the only tranche-2 item that would grow scope. Spec §11.10. **Residue:** two `Cancel` text buttons the BUTTON half missed (`QuickChips.tsx:374`, `RatingSubmitModal.tsx:345`) — both `text-[10px]` where the nearest primitive step is 11px, so left rather than silently resized; two hardcoded-blue spinners in SyncSetupWizard; and three queued eyeball checks (four `text-sm` fields became `text-xs` — a visible shrink on two marketplace search boxes; the `panel-glass` nav chips; a sync toggle's `cursor-wait`→`cursor-not-allowed` while provisioning). AccountSection's Save height mismatch is structurally resolved — those buttons now live inside the field's own wrapper. Implementation log: **spec §13**. Tranches 3–8 remain — **resume from `docs/archive/handoffs/2026-07-19-ui-consistency-tranche-3-handoff.md`**, which carries the open contrast decision, the queued eyeball checks, the residue list, and a warning that tranche 3's stated scope looks understated the same way tranches 1–2 were (greps suggest ~58 `bg-panel`+border sites and ~54 `bg-well` sites against changes 22/25's handful of named components — upper bounds, not counts; audit before implementing). Also verified 2026-07-19: **ESLint does not exist in this repo** (no config, no lint script), so §8's "ESLint guardrail" is adopt-from-zero, not add-a-rule — size it accordingly. **Update 2026-07-23 — tranches 3 + 5 (partial) COMMITTED to branch `feat/ui-consistency-tranche-3` (UNPUSHED, no PR yet — not merged to master):** **Tranche 3** (cards 22–25, commit `1f68a7f0`) + a new `.hover-lift` CSS guard that puts the card scale behind `@media (hover: hover)` — the guard didn't exist and three cards shipped a bare `hover:scale-[1.02]` that stuck after a tap on the Android WebView (behavior fix, retro-applied to MarketplaceCard + both FilesTab cards). The §14 audit found the ledger **overstated** (opposite of tranches 1–2): `SkillCard`'s `marketplace` variant was **dead code** (only importer is CommandDrawer, never passed the prop; LibraryScreen's lookalike renders a MarketplaceCard) — deleted, not migrated, taking 4 of change 23's 6 hexes with it; change 22's real grid was **3 sibling card species** in one drawer grid (2 unledgered), not one; a **second, drifted** `locked` tone map existed in MarketplaceScreen; and change 25's `bg-well` idiom had **3 identical copies** in one panel, not the one named. Rulings: dead branch deleted; star gold stays a raw hex (app-store convention, no `--gold` token exists) as one shared `STAR_GOLD_CLASS`; "Published successfully!" goes neutral (errors-are-neutral logic, design rule 6). **Tranche 5** (states) — **change 34** (`c6590224`): error-MESSAGE text → `text-destructive-fg` at 42 sites/23 files. **Ledger token correction**: the spec said `text-destructive`, but text must use `text-destructive-fg` (the fill token is too dark to read as text on dark themes; all 11 prior sites + FieldError use `-fg`) — following §1.6 literally would reintroduce the contrast bug the split fixed. Excluded (verified): status scales/tones, status glyphs, danger-action affordances, games (change 47), diff/tool-output tones, git-stat metrics, red-tinted boxes (change 33's job). **Changes 31/32** (`9634e58d`): adopted `LoadingState` (0→8) + `EmptyState` (0→2); each loader now names what it loads; ResumeBrowser's empty gains a Clear-search action. Left deliberately: the two "quiet skeleton, not a spinner" loaders (Providers protected by name in §2), project-view tab loads (block would center them), and LibraryScreen `Section`/ReviewList's own clean empty abstractions. **Change 33 (ErrorState) HELD** — its per-site recoverable-vs-general split is the error-message audit's own core decision; the component is built and ready, which is all "tranche 5 unblocks the audit" ever meant. Impl logs: **spec §14 (§14.11) + §15**. **Update 2026-07-23 (same branch) — tranche 7 (form controls 38–40) COMMITTED (`f779c3f0` + `06d96ccf`).** This turns the LAST zero-call-site primitives live: Radio, RadioGroup, and Checkbox now have call sites, and the renderer has **zero native `<input type=radio|checkbox>`**. **39** — Radio at all 3 native-radio sites (PreferencesPopup permission list; NOT a one-word swap (currentRoundness derives from the theme file via async writeFile, so a bare `value=` freezes the thumb mid-drag) — fixed with a draft+resync effect like EngineCard's context knob. **Checkbox** — adopted at ProjectView's delete-consent checkbox (ledger :807 → actually :846). **38** (structural) — SessionDrawer's native sort `<select>` + two CheckboxGlyph rows collapse into one sliders trigger opening the shared FileFilterPopover ("filter menu like project view"); 37 = creme.json contrast, a real bug shipping today, pull it out early), 8 (session-8 41–51 — CloseButton half of 41 already done), plus change 33 (held for the error-message audit) and deferred change 78.** Remaining recommended order 4→8→6 (5 + 7 done; see the tranche-3 plan doc). **Update 2026-07-23 — MERGED to master: youcoded PR #245, merge `dd3e5b30`** (tranches 3 + 5[31/32/34] + 7[38/39/40], 14 commits; CI green on all three platforms; 3239 tests). **Every `components/ui/` primitive now has call sites** — before this branch seven shipped primitives had ZERO consumers, and the renderer now has zero native `<input type=radio|checkbox>`. Destin reviewed the whole branch in a live dev instance before merge; seven follow-ups came out of it and TWO reversed his own earlier rulings — full log in **spec §17**: the card-hover regression (`.layer-surface`'s unlayered background silently beats any competing Tailwind utility — the same trap that forced `!rounded-lg`; fixed with a named `.card-interactive`), no-results empty states with a way out across all three file surfaces (reversing the 2026-07-22 "the (0) headers carry it" ruling), "Session artifacts" → "Session Files" (3 sites + an INVERTED test guard that had pinned the opposite), a shared `SearchFilterPill` replacing two divergent search+filter controls that each carried their own sliders glyph, multi-select Type filter, and the retirement of "Hide code & configs" (removed down to theme-context, where it was a persisted+synced setting nothing read; also exposed that Project View used a LOCAL hideCode state while the drawer used the persisted context flag — one visible control, two drifting settings). **Behavior changes for release notes:** Android sticky-hover fixed (a tap used to leave cards stuck enlarged — three cards shipped an unguarded `hover:scale-[1.02]`); error text is now theme-derived so community packs can shift it (built-ins pixel-identical); **the default file view is no longer documents-first** — code/config files now appear by default in both browsers (Destin accepted; to restore, initialise the type selection to `{document,image,sheet}` rather than reviving the hide toggle); `RadioGroup` no longer hijacks arrow keys typed into a nested field. **REMAINING: tranches 4 (screens/nav 26–30), 8 (session-8 41–51), 6 (type/tokens 35–37 — the ~594-site rename; 37 = creme.json contrast, a real bug shipping today), plus change 33 (held for the error-message audit) and deferred change 78.** **Update 2026-07-24 — TRANCHE 8 MERGED (youcoded PR #252, `a5e6e8f1`; spec §20). THE MIGRATION IS COMPLETE — 49 of 51 shipped.** The audit again found the ledger stale in both directions: changes 42 (Textarea) and 43 (--on-destructive) had ALREADY shipped in tranches 2 and 0, and 41 was ~80% done. **The find: `FirstRunView` declared its own local `ProgressBar`** — a near-exact copy of the shared primitive — and that local declaration SHADOWED the import site, so the primitive read as adopted (a grep for the component name matched) while actually having zero consumers, and the file the spec called "already matched" was the reason nobody noticed. Second shadow-copy this workstream has found (tranche 3 killed SkillCard's dead `marketplace` variant) and the first where grepping the name actively hid the problem. Guarded now: `tests/primitive-adoption.test.ts` fails if any `components/ui/` primitive has no call site outside `ui/`. **Two things the Toast ledger missed:** the primitive is `pointer-events-none` (so it never swallows clicks meant for the app underneath) but App's toast carries a real clickable "Send anyway" action — routing it through `message` would have shipped a DEAD button, so it gained an explicit `pointer-events-auto` action slot; and the 14 manual `setTimeout`s did NOT agree on a duration (3s/4s/6s/8s, scaled to reading length — the 8s ones are the cross-device handoff warnings), so a single primitive default would have silently cut those to 3s. Duration moved onto the toast state; all 14 timers deleted. **Change 50 was approved, built, and REVERSED on sight** — Destin rejected the headerless Settings drawer in the dev instance on 2026-07-24. Esc and click-outside do work (verified before the ✕ came out), but the title row is what tells you which drawer you are in and the ✕ is what a non-technical user reaches for; **design rule 12 gets NO Settings-drawer exception, do not re-delete the header on the strength of the 2026-07-16 approval.** The reusable half is the macOS coupling: the header carried `.mac-titlebar-inset .settings-drawer-header{padding-top:28px}`, the only thing keeping the first row clear of the native traffic lights — the guard now pins the PAIR (it reads which anchor SettingsPanel uses and requires globals.css to pad that same selector), because breaking it is invisible on Linux and Windows. **Judgment calls made without asking:** ConnectFour's piece colours stay hardcoded (`state.myColor === 'red'` is a wire-protocol value, not styling — theming it desyncs the board from game state); ThemeScreen's swatch button uses `bg-current/15` rather than the ghost Button's `hover:bg-inset`, because it floats on a swatch painted in the PREVIEWED theme's colours where an app-theme fill can land invisible (this retires the app's last raw `hover:bg-black/20`); TerminalToolbar keeps 40×40 instead of `size="icon"`'s 28px since it is a primary touch control over a terminal. **Release-note behaviour changes:** friend-request Accept/Decline + Challenge are real buttons instead of coloured text (Accept was `text-green-400` — "yes" by colour alone, the exact pairing rule 5 retires); Library's inactive tabs lose their permanent fill and shrink a step; Settings rows read one step larger at the same height; the Settings ✕ is 28px with a focus ring and a real accessible name; the update panel finally shows a progress bar (download % previously existed only inside a disabled, dimmed button label); Toggles gain an invisible 44×44 Android touch target (desktop byte-identical); community-theme game-lobby links and errors now follow the theme. **The one rule worth carrying out of eight tranches: audit before editing, and treat a count in a spec as a hypothesis.** Spec archived to `docs/archive/specs/2026-07-16-ui-consistency-design-spec.md`. Remaining ledger items are tracked elsewhere: change 33 under the error-message-standards entry, change 78 as its own entry below. **Next: the whole-UI review pass entry below.** **Update 2026-07-24 — tranche 4 MERGED (youcoded PR #246, `cc1d3944`; spec §18):** `Overlay.tsx` is now the sole z-index authority (design rule 11 enforced by `tests/overlay-layer-authority.test.ts`). ProjectView dropped from `z-[8000]` to the shared `z-40` screen layer, which **un-swallowed toasts, context menus and info bubbles** that had been painting behind it; seven settings popups collapsed into one `SettingsPopup` and two byte-identical donate blocks into one `DonateConfirm` (−243 lines net). The §18 audit again found the ledger wrong in both directions: change 26's stated warning pointed at the right edit for the wrong reason, two clauses had already shipped, change 29 was half-wrong (ModelLoadingBar stays positioned in-column), change 30 needed component extraction rather than in-place swaps, and three unledgered magic z-indexes turned up. **Update 2026-07-24 — tranche 6 MERGED (youcoded PR #249; spec §19) — and the ledger had it split across two tranches without saying so.** Verified before editing: 35's `@theme` tokens, 36's `theme-types` optionality AND its whole `computeOverlayTokens` derivation, and **all of change 37** had already shipped in tranche 0 / the 2026-07-19 contrast rewrite — creme is at `#7D7161`/`#9A8F7F`, better than the values the spec proposed. **The "pull 37 forward, it's a live bug" advice carried in two handoffs was stale; there was no bug.** What was actually open: (a) the rename — 572 sites / 87 files, of which **546 are pixel-identical** (`text-[12px]`→`text-xs` is free: Tailwind's xs IS 12px and nothing overrides the root font-size). `text-[8px]` (2 sites) wasn't in the ledger at all. **DECIDED: `--text-sm-tight: 13px` is a new named token, not a fold** — all 22 of those sites are Project View body copy on the screen Destin signed off two days earlier, and folding to `text-sm` would have resized the whole screen for nothing; the 15/17/8px sites fold UP so titles keep their step. Guard `tests/type-scale-authority.test.ts`, mutation-verified. (b) 36's one unshipped clause, "the contrast audit gains a link check" — **which was hiding a shipping bug.** Building the check first and measuring before coding showed the tranche-0 derivation's `>40` accent-vs-fg guard only asks "distinguishable from body text", never "readable": **6 of 11 shipped themes fail AA on their link, including built-in `light`, worst `kuromi-dreamer` at 2.90:1.** Adding the rule as specced would have turned CI red on all six. **DECIDED: give `deriveLink` the same AA nudge `deriveDestructiveFg` already has, then the HARD rule is safe.** Surface set is canvas + panel + **inset** — the assistant bubble is `bg-inset` and `MarkdownContent` renders inside it, so the bubble is where links actually live; auditing canvas alone is the exact §12 mistake (canvas is the best case, so measuring it alone ships the worst). `well` excluded on purpose (command-drawer search surface, renders no links; gating on it fails two packs over a pairing the app can't produce). **Only DERIVED links are nudged** — a declared link is the author's choice (creme's olive is deliberate) and auto-correcting it would make the new rules unfailable. Built-in `light` fixed by hand (`#2563EB`→`#2055CA`, 3.59:1 on inset). Four community packs get a readable link; zero new failures vs a rule-by-rule baseline. **Also learned: the contrast rules file is vendored THREE ways** (canonical in wecoded-marketplace, copies in wecoded-themes + youcoded) and wecoded-themes CI diffs its copy against marketplace master — so merge order is mandatory (marketplace #64 → themes #25 → youcoded), and getting it wrong red-lights somebody else's theme submission rather than your own PR. **REMAINING: tranche 8 (session-8 41–51) ONLY, plus held change 33 and deferred change 78. Audit 41 before implementing — its CloseButton half shipped in tranche 2 and tranche 4 already collapsed seven settings popups, so its real scope is smaller than the ledger's.** On completion, see the whole-UI review pass entry below.
- [x] Session launch + model + tag UX overhaul — new-session menu, resume browser, Model Providers `feature` `#ui` `#renderer` `#sessions` `#native-runtime` (added 2026-07-29, **shipped 2026-07-31 — youcoded#279**)
  **Shipped.** All five findings below were addressed. One `ModelPicker` replaces the four model UIs; `NativeModelSelect.tsx` and `RuntimeBindingFields` are deleted, so the Runtime toggle no longer exists anywhere — the runtime is derived from the model picked. The resume row's expanded panel is launch-only, with tags/note moved to an in-card sheet reachable WITHOUT expanding (so it also reaches rows that can't be resumed on this device). Priority became a built-in, undeletable tag and Complete became a one-click check on the card, retiring the parallel flags-vs-tags shapes. `TagManagerPopup` is the central tag management that finding (5) says did not exist. Claude Code sessions now record their model so the resume card can show it. **Structural question answered:** New Session and Resume stay two surfaces — Destin's call, 2026-07-30.
  Design came out of ten rounds in the workbench comparison view; the surviving open items are tracked in `docs/archive/handoffs/2026-07-31-session-launch-ux-open-items.md` and the two entries added below.
  Destin's ask 2026-07-29, verbatim shape: he "quite dislike[s]" the new-session menu and the resume browser, wants both **simplified with better navigability**, wants **native model handling improved across those two and the Model Providers settings menu**, and wants the **tags and model-selection interfaces** cleaned up specifically. Nothing is designed yet — the session pivoted to building the workbench first so alternatives can be mocked in the real renderer. **The structural question is still open and unanswered:** should New Session and Resume stay two surfaces, become one launcher, or become one launcher plus a separate session-organizing surface? Destin hadn't considered merging them and wants to see proposals for both before deciding.
  **Findings from the 2026-07-29 source read (all verified, cite these rather than re-deriving them):**
  (1) **The new-session form exists twice** — `SessionStrip.tsx:999` (dropdown) and `App.tsx:2932` (welcome screen). They share `RuntimeBindingFields` but each keeps its own Claude-alias model row. Field stack is Folder → Runtime toggle → Provider → Model → Preset → *(or)* alias row → Skip Permissions → Launch in New Window → Create.
  (2) **Model selection has FOUR distinct UIs**: a 4-button alias row (new session, CC runtime); two stacked `<Select>`s for Provider then Model (`RuntimeBinding.tsx:252-298`, native); `NativeModelSelect.tsx` — search box + list grouped by provider (resume, native); and `ModelPickerPopup.tsx`'s own third native rendering (in-session). `NativeModelSelect`'s header comment says it was extracted FROM `ModelPickerPopup` — so two of the four are known duplicates that were never reconciled.
  (3) **The resume row's expanded panel mixes two unrelated jobs** — launch options (model, skip-perms, new-window) and metadata editing (Flags, Tags, Note) — as 7 stacked labelled fields injected into a list row (`ResumeBrowser.tsx:511-668`).
  (4) **Flags and Tags are parallel systems with different UI**: Priority/Complete are a hardcoded 2-button row (`FLAG_ORDER`, `ResumeBrowser.tsx:154`), custom tags are a `TagPicker`. The Tags *filter* is a third shape again (a checkbox dropdown, `:881-910`).
  (5) **No central tag management exists.** `TagPicker` — which owns rename, recolor, archive and delete — has exactly 3 call sites (`ResumeBrowser`, `CloseSessionPrompt`, `SessionTagsChip`); `useTagRegistry` has the same 3 consumers; SettingsPanel has no tag section, only a "show tag options when closing a session" toggle. So editing a tag requires expanding a past-session row. Verified by two searches plus a settings read, 2026-07-29.
  (6) **Live bug, fix on sight:** the two dead-end pointers disagree — `RuntimeBinding.tsx:249` says "Add a provider key in Settings → **Model Providers** first", `NativeModelSelect.tsx:144` says "Settings → **Providers**". Neither is a link, and only the first name is real.
  (7) Browser-only workbench caveat that bites this work directly: `detachAvailable` gates the "Launch in New Window" toggle in BOTH forms (`SessionStrip.tsx:1068`, `ResumeBrowser.tsx:584`), so the mock must fake `detach.openDetached` or that control vanishes from the surfaces under redesign. Already handled in the workbench plan's Task 4.
- [x] UI Workbench — build every new surface against a mock backend before the backend exists `feature` `#ui` `#renderer` `#tests` (added 2026-07-29; SHIPPED 2026-07-30, youcoded PR #273 `9fa50b14`). **Two deltas from the plan below.** The toolbar has no variant switcher — the registry would have shipped with one entry, zero call sites and a switcher that did not re-render, so it is deferred to the first real two-candidate comparison. And `stress` seeds data only; slowness is a separate latency control (0/150ms/2s), because timing — not data shape — is the failure mode UI-first development actually introduces. Also fixed en route, and NOT workbench-only: `resolveAssetPath` only passed through `theme-asset://`, so any theme shipping a `data:` URI, a remote image or a root-absolute path resolved to a dead link **in the real app too**.
  Destin's decision 2026-07-29: **all future feature UI gets built in a live mockup of the app first, then the backend behind it.** A dev-only `?mode=workbench` boots the REAL renderer in a plain browser tab (Vite only — no Electron, no PTY, no main process) against a fake `window.claude`, so every menu is reachable, interactive and stateful. Because it runs the real components against the real `globals.css` and theme engine, "the app takes on the exact format of the mockup" stops being a goal and becomes structurally unavoidable — there is no second copy to translate. Design alternatives live side by side as real components behind a toolbar variant switcher; picking a winner means deleting the losers, not porting anything. **Supersedes `docs/archive/plans/2026-07-20-mockup-browser-renderer.md`** (draft, never built, never tracked here — which is most of why it sat): that plan made conversations live and menus inert, and today's purpose is the inverse. Adopted from it: the `Proxy` catch-all so unimplemented channels warn-once and resolve empty instead of hanging 30s, the Vite-only launcher, hydrate snapshots as data rather than event scripts, and the parity guard. Its "resist stubbing namespaces for completeness" rule is retired and replaced by a `MOCK_ONLY` registry — every hand-written channel either mirrors a real `preload.ts` channel or is registered against the unbuilt feature it belongs to, so a fake can never quietly ship as real **and the registry doubles as the generated backend to-do list.** The typed `Window.claude` contract already exists (`renderer/hooks/useIpc.ts:17`, 21 namespaces), so the mock is compiler-checked; the `(window as any).claude` call sites that escape it (ResumeBrowser's `browse`/`setFlag`/`setTag`/`setNote`, App's `firstRun`/`detach`, all of `theme`) get fixed opportunistically as each surface is touched. **Also deletes the ToolCard sandbox** (`?mode=tool-sandbox`, `run-sandbox.sh`, `ToolSandbox.tsx`, `fixture-loader.ts`) — its 24 `.jsonl` fixtures are absorbed as a workbench tool-gallery surface, which is the "catalog" in phase 1 rather than a follow-up. **And rewrites the `ui-mockup` skill**: the rendering half (hand-reimplemented Tailwind utilities, token tables, `computeOverlayTokens` recipes, glass-cascade rules, artifact publishing) is deleted because the real CSS now supplies it; the process half survives intact — numbered changes, change-ledger, approve-by-number, before/after on real surfaces, Halftone Dimension as the stress theme, explicit fidelity notes. **One risk designed against:** identical appearance is guaranteed, identical *behaviour under real data* is not — so a `stress` scenario (200 sessions, 80-char names, missing fields, a timing-out provider) ships first-class, and no surface is approved until it has been seen under `stress` and `empty`, not just the happy path. Phase 2 (separate entry when wanted) is the superseded plan's live play-through replayer. Spec: `docs/archive/specs/2026-07-29-ui-workbench-design.md`.

BUDDY-FLOATER TRACK SHIPPED 2026-07-16 (youcoded PR #159, merge e63fa96e; plan Tasks 1–13 complete + archived): action bar, living rig on the floater (unified springs, five motion styles, per-style blinks, hyper twitches, drag trailing), chat open/close animation, edge snap + dock/peek with the approved 75°-wider side-peek (lean layer + edge-pinned mittens), dismiss-for-run + settings status. Scene companions SHIPPED for the welcome screen (MascotScene static tier; wecoded-themes PR #17 — sun/motes/sparks/berries/ghost as a top-level `companions` manifest key, themes at 1.2.0, side-loaded). FEEDBACK TUNING SHIPPED 2026-07-17 (youcoded merge 18d8ac0d, seven eyeball rounds against a live dev instance): quick actions now pop in left-to-right on chat open only (hover reveal deleted, along with the whole renderer→main hover IPC path), bouncier chat entrance, all group geometry consolidated into the pure+tested `buddy-bar-geometry.ts` (the bar's left/right flip was proven unreachable and removed), the mascot/chat/bar move as one rigid group with the chat's fit constraining the mascot's x, gaps measured to the rig's ink rather than window edges, bar lined up with the hands, and peek reworked from an idle timer to drag-driven (snap on release at an edge, hop out briefly on hover). Invariants folded into docs/PITFALLS.md § Buddy Floater. PEEK REFINEMENTS SHIPPED 2026-07-17 (youcoded PR #166 merge ae9aa03a; wecoded-themes PR #18 merge 9f770f6; wecoded-marketplace PR #44; full desktop suite 2609 green): side-peek grip retuned (mittens straddle the leaning body, body between the hands); peek hands re-extract on theme switch (MutationObserver + rigUrl key); the rig's own arms hide in side-peek (new PartPose `hidden`) so no phantom third arm; hover-out is now a held swing (out while cursor's on him, sink back on leave) instead of a 700ms hop; peek ENGAGES LIVE mid-drag (new `drag-peek` dock event — snap while held, slide along the edge, hysteresis); chat-open drag no longer false-snaps into peek on release, and shoving past the edge with the chat open closes the chat+bar (animated) and hands off to peek; hoverArmed latch stops an instant pop when you drop into peek under the cursor. Rig-contract audit (wecoded-themes/scripts/audit-rigs.mjs) added as BLOCKING CI; all six skin templates + halftone-dimension peek-hand styling fixed. Remaining follow-up tracked below: buddy-floater companion FOLLOW physics (window-padding redesign).
- [x] Games arcade — Connect 4 becomes four games `feature` `#games` `#ui` (added 2026-08-30, shipped 2026-08-31)
  Turns the Connect 4 panel into a four-game arcade (Connect 4, Chess, Flappy, 2048) with friend leaderboards for the solo games and head-to-head records for the versus ones.
  **SHIPPED 2026-08-31.** Server: wecoded-marketplace#78 (merged + auto-deployed to Cloudflare; migration `0007_games.sql`). App: youcoded#369 (`0cacff56`, 18 commits). Docs archived: `docs/archive/specs/2026-08-30-games-arcade-design.md`, `docs/archive/design/2026-08-30-games-arcade/decisions.md`, `docs/archive/handoffs/2026-08-31-games-arcade-handoff.md`.
  **Three bugs were found in review and fixed before merge**, all with guards confirmed RED against the unfixed code first: (1) a settled head-to-head record was stored unconditionally and painted next to whoever was on screen — the presence socket fans every frame to EVERY window and every remote browser, and the Worker settles up to two minutes late, so your Connect 4 record vs Mira could caption a chess win over Jake; MATCH_RECORDED now carries (opponent, matchId) and the reducer drops anything not for the match on screen. (2) Committing a games-pane resize re-ran the effect that stops play, destroying an in-progress Flappy/2048 run with the score uncounted — the width callback's identity was unstable; now `useCallback([])` plus a separate effect keyed on `openGame` alone. (3) A source-guard in the branch derived a filename with `split('/')`, which is the wrong separator on Windows, so it failed on Windows CI naming the two files it exists to permit — green on Linux and macOS the whole time.
  **Merged with a red macOS leg, knowingly** (Destin's call): the failure is `sync-spaces-engine.test.ts`, the long-open macOS bug at ROADMAP :619, in a subsystem this PR does not touch (verified pre-merge; the branch is deleted, so re-check with `git show --stat 0cacff56`). Ubuntu and Windows were green. See :619 for today's evidence, including the load-correlation this PR adds.
  **Not verified by eye this session:** the merge was reviewed by reading code and running tests. The arcade screens, chess board, end-of-run card and animations were signed off earlier via review decks but were not re-checked in a running app before merge.
